\"\"\"Merge adjacent text elements so any {{...}} spans live in one element.\n\n    Returns True if any consolidation change was made.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Build the concatenated paragraph string from all <t> elements.\n    - Find all placeholder spans in that concatenated string.\n    - For any placeholder span that crosses element boundaries, merge the entire\n      text of the covered prevents overlapping characters.\n\n    Returns number of removed elements.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    removed = 0\n\n    # These are typically direct children of <hp:p>, but we'll be conservative and\n    # (lineSegArray/linesegarray variants) from any\n  paragraph whose text changed\n- Verify no placeholders remain\n- Repackage as /root/event_announcement_ready.hwpx\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nimport zipfile\nfrom xml.etree import ElementTree as ET\n\nBASE_DIR = \"/tmp/hwpx_work/extracted\"\nDATA_PATH = \"/root/event_data.json\"\nOUTPUT_PATH = \"/root/event_announcement_ready.hwpx\"\n\nPLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{(.+?)\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef iter_xml_files_with_placeholders(base_dir: str) -> list[str]:\n    hits: list[str] = []\n    for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(base_dir):\n        for fn in files:\n            if not fn.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                continue\n            full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            try:\n                with open(full, \"rb\") as f:\n                    blob = f.read()\n            except OSError:\n                continue\n  ElementTree serializes with 'ns0' prefixes unless namespaces are registered.\n    We collect them from the document and register before writing.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    nsmap: dict[str, str] = {}\n    # iterparse emits ('start-ns', (prefix, uri))\n    for _event, (prefix, uri) in ET.iterparse(xml_path, events=(\"start-ns\",)):\n        # prefix can be '' for default namespace\n        if prefix in nsmap:\n            continue\n        nsmap[prefix] = uri\n    return nsmap\n\n\ndef register_namespaces(nsmap: dict[str, str]) -> None:\n    for prefix, uri in nsmap.items():\n        # ElementTree expects '' for default namespace; None is not accepted.\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef paragraph_text_elems(p: ET.Element) -> list[ET.Element]:\n    \"\"\"Return all <...:t> descendants in document order within a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    out: list[ET.Element] = []\n    for el in p.iter():\n        if localname(el.tag) == \"t\":\n            out.append(el)\n    return out\n\n\ndef consolidate_fragmented_placeholders_in_paragraph(p: ET.Element) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Merge adjacent text elements so any {{...}} spans live in one element.\n\n    Returns True if any consolidation change was made.\n\n    Strategy:\n    - Build the concatenated paragraph string from all <t> elements.\n    - Find all placeholder spans in that concatenated string.\n    - For any placeholder span that crosses element boundaries, merge the entire\n      text of the covered element range into the first element and blank out the\n      subsequent elements in that range.\n\n    This preserves non-placeholder text outside the span and keeps modification\n    localized to the smallest element range that contains the placeholder.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    t_elems = paragraph_text_elems(p)\n    if len(t_elems) <= 1:\n        return False\n\n    texts = [(te.text or \"\") for te in t_elems]\n    combined = \"\".join(texts)\n    if \"{{\" not in combined:\n        return False\n\n    # Build ranges mapping for char offsets.\n    ranges: list[tuple[int, int]] = []\n    pos = 0\n    for s in texts:\n        start = pos\n        end = pos + len(s)\n        ranges.append((start, end))\n        pos = end\n\n    def elem_index_for_offset(offset: int) -> int:\n        # offset is in [0, len(combined)]\n        # For end offsets, we use offset-1 logic elsewhere.\n        for i, (a, b) in enumerate(ranges):\n            if a <= offset < b:\n                return i\n        # If offset is exactly at end, map to last.\n        return len(ranges) - 1\n\n    changed = False\n\n    # Iterate matches from left to right. We'll recompute combined/ranges when we mutate.\n    # Mutations only merge elements and blank others; cumulative offsets remain valid if\n    # we recompute per merge.\n    while True:\n        texts = [(te.text or \"\") for te in t_elems]\n        combined = \"\".join(texts)\n        if \"{{\" not in combined:\n            break\n\n        # Find the first placeholder that crosses boundaries.\n        crossing = None\n        for m in PLACEHOLDER_RE.finditer(combined):\n            s, e = m.span()\n            if s == e:\n                continue\n            i = elem_index_for_offset(s)\n            j = elem_index_for_offset(e - 1)\n            if i != j:\n                crossing = (i, j)\n                break\n\n        if not crossing:\n            break\n\n        i, j = crossing\n\n        # Merge i..j into element i.\n        merged_text = \"\".join((t_elems[k].text or \"\") for k in range(i, j + 1))\n        if (t_elems[i].text or \"\") != merged_text:\n            t_elems[i].text = merged_text\n            changed = True\n        for k in range(i + 1, j + 1):\n            if (t_elems[k].text or \"\") != \"\":\n                t_elems[k].text = \"\"\n                changed = True\n\n    return changed\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_paragraph(p: ET.Element, data: dict[str, object]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders in all <t> elements. Returns True if any change.\"\"\"\n    changed = False\n    for te in paragraph_text_elems(p):\n        before = te.text or \"\"\n        if \"{{\" not in before:\n            continue\n\n        def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n            key = m.group(1)\n            if key in data:\n                return str(data[key])\n            return m.group(0)\n\n        after = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, before)\n        if after != before:\n            te.text = after\n            changed = True\n    return changed\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_children(p: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove line segment cache elements inside a paragraph.\n\n    HWPX may cache layout in elements like <hp:linesegarray> or <hp:lineSegArray>.\n    Removing them after text changes prevents overlapping characters.\n\n    Returns number of removed elements.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    removed = 0\n\n    # These are typically direct children of <hp:p>, but we'll be conservative and\n    # remove any matching direct children.\n    for child in list(p):\n        ln = localname(child.tag)\n        if ln in {\"linesegarray\", \"lineSegArray\", \"lineSegarray\", \"linesegArray\"}:\n            p.remove(child)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path: str, data: dict[str, object]) -> tuple[bool, int]:\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file.\n\n    Returns (file_changed, paragraphs_modified_count)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    nsmap = collect_namespaces(xml_path)\n    register_namespaces(nsmap)\n\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    file_changed = False\n    modified_paras = 0\n\n    # Identify paragraph elements by localname == 'p'\n    for p in root.iter():\n        if localname(p.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        # Step 1: consolidate placeholders spanning multiple <t>\n        consolidated = consolidate_fragmented_placeholders_in_paragraph(p)\n\n        # Step 2: replace placeholders\n        replaced = replace_placeholders_in_paragraph(p, data)\n\n        if consolidated or replaced:\n            # Remove layout cache elements when paragraph text changed.\n            remove_layout_cache_children(p)\n            file_changed = True\n            modified_paras += 1\n\n    if file_changed:\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return file_changed, modified_paras\n\n\ndef assert_no_placeholders_remain(base_dir: str) -> None:\n    remaining: list[str] = []\n    for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(base_dir):\n        for fn in files:\n            if not fn.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                continue\n            full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            with open(full, \"rb\") as f:\n                blob = f.read()\n            if b\"{{\" in blob:\n                remaining.append(full)\n\n    if remaining:\n        raise SystemExit(\n            \"Placeholders remain in XML files:\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(sorted(remaining))\n        )\n\n\ndef repackage_hwpx(base_dir: str, output_path: str) -> None:\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, \"w\", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(base_dir):\n            for fn in files:\n                full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(full, base_dir)\n                zf.write(full, arcname)\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    with open(DATA_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    xml_files = iter_xml_files_with_placeholders(BASE_DIR)\n    if not xml_files:\n        print(\"No XML files with placeholders found.\")\n\n    total_modified_paras = 0\n    modified_files = 0\n    for xml_path in sorted(xml_files):\n        file_changed, | changed={file_changed} | modified_paras={modified_paras}\")\n\n    assert_no_placeholders_remain(BASE_DIR)\n\n    repackage_hwpx(BASE_DIR, OUTPUT_PATH)\n    print(f\"Wrote output: {OUTPUT_PATH}\")\n
